---- CORRECTED FIX:
The lane, the file and the reader are all correct; two shape corrections are needed.

1) toggle-modes.json is an id-KEYED OBJECT, not an array of rows. import-core-v2.mjs:642 reads it as `Object.entries(...)` and spreads `{ ...m, id }`, so "add a row {id:...}" would not load. The entry must be keyed:

"item-camouflaging-chromatophores-lesser": {
  "id": "item-camouflaging-chromatophores-lesser",
  "name": "Background Adaptation",
  "fromItemId": "camouflaging-chromatophores-lesser",
  "duration": "1 minute",
  "modifiers": [],
  "note": "You can Hide without needing cover or concealment. This does NOT let you Sneak without ending your movement in cover or concealment."
}

(inner "id" is redundant but matches the shipped item-bioluminescent-stripes entry; omit `category`, omit `survivesRest` - a 1-minute state correctly clears on rest by default per Ruling E continued, point 3.)

2) The identical printed clause sits on two sibling records with the identical absence, and a lesser-only fix leaves them broken: add the same keyed entries for "item-camouflaging-chromatophores-greater" (fromItemId 'camouflaging-chromatophores-greater', same note, duration "1 minute") and "item-camouflaging-chromatophores" (fromItemId 'camouflaging-chromatophores'). Both already carry activationCost + a frequency counter (the parent's 1/day frequency is itself an effect-backfill row at line ~74960), so both render the Activate control the moment the mode exists.

3) The SECONDARY trait claim is REAL but names no carrier, and a bare core.json edit is wiped by the next `npm run data`. Confirmed: core.trait has 'graft' (trait-790) and no 'gra'; 28 items carry the truncated 'gra' while only 4 carry 'graft'. `traits` is already a legal overlay field (174 existing rows in effect-backfill.json) and no traits row exists for this id, so the durable per-record form is:
{"category":"items","id":"camouflaging-chromatophores-lesser","field":"traits","value":["graft","invested","magical"]}
Because the truncation hits 28 items from one import path, prefer fixing the trait normalization in the importer and regenerating over authoring 28 overlay rows; the overlay row above is the correct fallback if only this record is in scope.

FIX IS RIGHT-BUT-MISSPECCED on four points, hence correctedFix:
1. "Then drop the dead star at situationalBonuses.ts:1501" is affirmatively WRONG and would create a new defect. 3. The sanctioned authoring pattern is the append-in-place one in scripts/add-sea-touch-modes.mjs, whose header documents that a mode living anywhere but its source file "is deleted by the next regen" (how 428 modes once vanished).
4. "Clause 2 has no carrier at all" is FALSE. The full sentence is present verbatim in public/core-descriptions.json items["phantom-roll"].d, and DECIDED SCOPE holds that a prose description is delivery for play-time procedure text. It belongs in the mode note as a nicety (as auric-noodles does), not as a claimed missing lane.

Editing :2543 in place is safe despite sitting inside the apply-situational-lane.mjs generated span (marker at :631): that script computes existingIds from the registry and skips any id already present, so a re-run cannot clobber it; the entry was itself hand-added into that span later (commit ecdea09, "Answer the 198 escalated questions"), and stoney-deflection at :679 already carries dcOnly:true inside the same span — precedent both ways. Twin shape anti-dragon-barding (:2642) uses the identical two-target dcOnly form.

Not ASK-OWNER: no WG-vs-print disagreement on this clause — our own bonus prose already reads "+1 item to your Fortitude DC (not to your Fortitude save)", i.e. WG's reading matches print exactly; only the machine-readable flag that routes the star is absent. work/owner-questions.json has zero entries mentioning golden-greaves, so the record is not owner-held.

Scope clean under docs/mechanic-lanes.md: this is a conditional bonus on the CHARACTER'S own defenses, delivered as situational stars (a star IS delivery), so no Ruling F (ally-only), no Ruling M (aura), no GM adjudication. The finding demands no new lane. Its parking of the misfortune reaction is also right: degreeShiftRecords (src/rules/explain.ts:311-331) collects degreeShifts off records the character owns and both consumers apply them to the character's OWN rolls, so an effect that turns an ENEMY's failure into a critical failure has no lane and correctly stays prose in core-descriptions.json.

Only nit, not a misspec: after adding dcOnly the parenthetical "(not to your Fortitude save)" in the bonus text becomes redundant with the sd-sit-dc placement (anti-dragon-barding ships bare "+2 item"). Harmless and still readable, so the fix as written is exactly right.

  "fromItemId": "hunters-brooch",
  "name": "Erastil's Steadying Hand",
  "duration": "1 minute",
  "modifiers": [],
  "weaponTraits": { "match": {}, "add": ["deadly-d12"] },

Against undead the weapon gains fatal d12 instead of deadly d12. Ends early if you score a critical hit with the weapon or stop wielding it. So the finding's "THE WHOLE STAFF IS MISSING / casts nothing" overstates the state of the app, and applying its fix as written leaves TWO working Spore Shepherd staff families in the picker. The fix must also add 'spore-shephards-staff', 'spore-shephards-staff-greater', 'spore-shephards-staff-major' to NEAR_DUPLICATE_IDS in src/data/index.ts — the exact 'exemplar-resilency' precedent already documented there ("A MISSPELLING, not a variant") — keeping the correctly-spelled ids that scripts/lib/wg-parse.mjs:223 and UMBRELLA_KEEP_IDS (src/data/index.ts:306+) both already name canonical. IN SCOPE, not owner-held, not a WG disagreement. Not ally-only (Ruling F), not an aura (M), not GM adjudication, not an internal activity step. work/owner-questions.json has no entry for this record. work/wg/wg-data.sql encodes three addBonusToValue rows (SAVE_FORT/REFLEX/WILL +1 item, restriction in `text`) and NOTHING for Breathe Clean — that is silence, not a contradiction of print, and WG has no modes concept at all, so a mode is not a divergence from their encoding. Owner Ruling E (work/rulings/DECISIONS.md:121) is directly on point in his own words: "create modes for all of the items that effect the user temporary and when you use them the mode activates."

WHY THE PROPOSED FIX IS WRONG. The finding never considered the modes lane, which is the exact-fit carrier and already built: ModeDef.immunities (types.ts:3999) is consumed by deriveDefenses (derive.ts:2444-2446, pushed under the mode's name); a non-consumable item mode gets a live Activate/Stop button with its duration printed beside it (ItemDetail.tsx:192, 449-462 → play.ts toggleItemMode:1459); and it self-clears on rest because survivesRest is absent (play.ts:2010-2012). The precedent is exact — `item-grim-specter` is a once-per-day EQUIPMENT relic whose timed activation ships as an item mode with `immunities` plus a prose `note`; nine more worn/tattooed/held equipment items carry activation modes in consumable-modes.json, and 1-round durations are established (item-shielding-salve, dragons-blood-pudding-*), as are display-only `modifiers: []` modes (item-deadweight-snare).

Against that, a FEAT_SITUATIONAL row is wrong in kind on three counts: (1) mechanic-lanes.md defines `situational` as "a bonus that applies only on a specific roll or in a specific circumstance" and Ruling B as "a flat bonus the rules restrict" — an IMMUNITY is not a bonus on a roll (when you are immune you do not roll the save), and there is no prior art: "immune" appears in a `bonus` string nowhere in situationalBonuses.ts's 3,000-odd rows. (2) A star is permanent furniture on all three save rows, sitting directly beside the existing +1 star whose trigger clause is the same three keywords, so the sheet would show two near-identical permanent entries for a once-per-day 1-round burst. (3) The proposed `bonus` string is 134 characters, which is precisely the failure the codebase documents in its own comment above blowgun-poisoner (situationalBonuses.ts:198-203): cap-situational-notes.mjs only rewrites an over-long `when`, "so a 141-character `bonus` would render as a paragraph inside a list item for ever." The "breathe normally underwater / in a vacuum" half is also not a saving throw at all and does not belong inside a save star's text.

The finding's cited precedents do not rescue it: emerald-fulcrum-lens and warding-tattoo do use "for 1 round after Activating (⟨N⟩, once/day)" in a `when`, but every one of those carries a NUMERIC typed bonus that has nowhere else to live; blowgun-poisoner's prose riders are on strike/skill targets, not a defence the app models as a field. Here the field exists.

Right defect, wrong shape → CONFIRMED with correctedFix. No collisions: effect-backfill.json has zero rows for this id, fixes.json and items-additions.json have none, apply-import-damaged-text.mjs has no REPAIRS row (the description is intact and complete, so no description repair is owed), and no mode id `item-gas-mask-of-clean-air` or fromItemId for this item exists in either mode file or in core.modes.

WHY THE FIX IS WRONG (two independent failures):

(1) IT DOES NOT DELIVER THE +1 — the exact clause it claims to fix. effectiveWeaponRunes reads the built-in floor as `db.items[inv.itemId]?.builtInRunes` (derive.ts:2999), and the combination reader derives the second Strike as `deriveStrike(c, db, { ...inv, itemId: meleeId, ... })` (derive.ts:4533) — `inv.itemId` is OVERWRITTEN with the melee-form id. So the lookup targets `reinforced-stock`, which has no builtInRunes, and `potencyBonus = Math.max(abp, runes?.potency ?? 0, …)` (derive.ts:3990) lands at 0. The finding's own justification ("the pistol's +1 … reach the stock Strike") is false: the derive comment says runes "all come off `inv`", which is true only of ETCHED runes (inv.runes). Measured across the corpus: all 18 existing `combinationMeleeForm` records have `builtInRunes === undefined` on BOTH halves — every one of them is a mundane weapon, so this hole has never been exercised. pistol-wand would be the first case, and it is precisely the case that breaks. (Player-etched striking/property runes WOULD flow, so the fix half-works — but "the pistol's potency rune" is the named half and it does not.)

(2) WRONG FILE. There is already an overlay row `{category:'items', id:'pistol-wand', field:'grantsItems', value:[{itemId:'reinforced-stock'}]}` in scripts/data/effect-backfill.json (plus a `builtInRunes` row; exactly 2 rows for this id). public/core.json is a build artefact — scripts/apply-backfill-now.mjs's header says the overlay is replayed into it and "the next full `npm run data` reproduces exactly this state from the same file". Editing core.json alone is reverted by the next rebuild and would fail overlay-durability drift.

Non-issues I checked and cleared: findCombinationFormIds (src/data/index.ts:421) keys on `formOf`, not on combinationMeleeForm, so nothing would have wrongly hidden `reinforced-stock` from the shop; no overlay row exists for items/pistol-wand/combinationMeleeForm, so no collision.

Right defect, wrong fix → CONFIRMED with correctedFix.
---- CORRECTED FIX:
The mechanism (`combinationMeleeForm`, reader derive.ts:4528-4535, one inventory row → two Strikes) is the right lane. But it must point at a DEDICATED usage record that carries the +1 itself, and it must be authored in the overlay, not in the build artefact.

In scripts/data/effect-backfill.json (NOT public/core.json):

A) ADD a create row for the usage record — same shape as the 18 existing `-melee` forms (see items.axe-musket-melee / items.crescent-cross-melee), stats mirrored from `reinforced-stock`, plus the built-in potency and `formOf`:
{ "category": "items", "id": "pistol-wand-stock", "create": true, "value": {
  "id": "pistol-wand-stock",
  "name": "Pistol Wand (Melee)",
  "level": 4,
  "price": { "gp": 100 },
  "bulk": 0.1,
  "traits": ["finesse", "two-hand-d8", "magical"],
  "rarity": "rare",
  "source": { "book": "Pathfinder NPC Core", "license": "ORC" },
  "usage": "held-in-one-hand",
  "itemType": "weapon",
  "hands": 1,
  "category": "martial",
  "group": "club",
  "damage": { "dice": 1, "die": "d4", "type": "bludgeoning" },
  "edition": "remaster-era",
  "builtInRunes": { "potency": 1 },
  "formOf": "pistol-wand"
} }
`builtInRunes` on THIS record is the load-bearing part — it is what makes "the pistol's potency rune … applies to Strikes with the stock as well" actually reach the second Strike, because effectiveWeaponRunes (derive.ts:2999) resolves the floor off the swapped `inv.itemId`. `formOf: "pistol-wand"` keeps it out of the shop via findCombinationFormIds (src/data/index.ts:421) — do NOT reuse the plain `reinforced-stock` id, which is a real 2 sp purchasable weapon that must stay buyable and must NOT gain a free +1. Etched striking/property runes ("and any other runes") already flow for free off the shared inventory row. No aonId is required on a create row (precedent: items.extradimensional-stash-space).

B) ADD: { "category": "items", "id": "pistol-wand", "field": "combinationMeleeForm", "value": "pistol-wand-stock" }  (no existing row for this category/id/field — verified no collision).

C) EDIT the existing pistol-wand `grantsItems` row IN PLACE to remove the field rather than deleting the row: { "category": "items", "id": "pistol-wand", "field": "grantsItems", "value": null }. `value: null` means REMOVE (scripts/lib/apply-backfill.mjs: "`value: null` means REMOVE the field"). Just deleting the row is not enough — apply-backfill-now.mjs replays against the shipped core.json and would leave the stale `grantsItems` (and its duplicate unequipped stock row) in place until a full `npm run data`.

Then run `node scripts/apply-backfill-now.mjs` (and `npm run data` before shipping).

Note (harness, not delivery): scripts/wg-identity.mjs:245 derives the counterpart name from the BASE record's name, so after this change our identity set offers "Pistol Wand (Melee)"/"Pistol Wand (Ranged)" where WG's giveItem says "Reinforced Stock". wg-diff may report a name-match gap for this record; that is a parity-harness artefact, not a delivery regression.

Alternative if a create row is unwanted: change derive.ts:4533 so the melee-form Strike inherits the BASE record's builtInRunes (e.g. pass the base item's builtInRunes through, or merge them in effectiveWeaponRunes) — then `combinationMeleeForm: "reinforced-stock"` as originally specified would work. That is an engine change and affects all 18 existing forms, so the data-only route above is the smaller correct one.

WHY THE FIX AS WRITTEN IS WRONG — THREE COUNTS, THE FIRST DECISIVE.

(1) WRONG LANE, AND IT BREAKS AN OWNER RULING. The clause is re-picked EVERY MORNING ("selected each morning as part of their daily preparations"). Ruling Q23, docs/gold-set-answers.md:250: daily choices "must **not** render in the builder. They appear when the player presses **Daily preparations**, and **default to the last choice made**." The proposed `passiveEffects.languageChoices: 1` is the PERMANENT bonus-language-slot lane: recordLanguageSlots (build.ts:1778-1815) → bonusLanguageSlots (build.ts:1818-1845) → consumed at src/builder/shared.tsx:3927, i.e. a BUILDER picker, answered once, permanent, and readable as a prerequisite for permanent character options. That is precisely the shape docs/mechanic-lanes.md:139 (`temporaryProficiency`) and scripts/apply-temporary-proficiency-lane.mjs exist to prevent. The printed analogue is Ancestral Linguistics (feat-1407, "become fluent in one common language … until you prepare again"), which core.json encodes as choice {flag:'ancestralLanguage', kind:'open', daily:true, from:{type:'language', grantLanguage:true}, note:'…'}.

(2) THE TYPES.TS EDIT IS UNNECESSARY AND POINTS AT THE WRONG INTERFACE'S JOB. ItemPassiveEffects (types.ts:2022-2071) needs no new field, because ItemBase ALREADY declares `choice?: FeatChoiceDef` (types.ts:3479), and items are first-class in the daily walk end to end: ownedDailyChoiceRecords pushes every equipped/worn/invested item (derive.ts:1209-1212 — "Items only count while actually in use"); dailyChoicesFor renders through that same walk (dailyChoices.ts:13 imports ownedDailyChoiceRecords aliased as ownedRecords) via openChoiceOptions (openChoice.ts:134-137); dailyChoiceGrants converts a grantLanguage answer into {grantsLanguages:[answer]} (derive.ts:1636-1638); applyPlayState merges it as `dailyLanguages` so the pill can say it lapses (play.ts:552-563). 20 items already carry `choice`, 10 of them `daily: true` (library-robes ×4, the-queen-mother, viridian-crown, shawl-of-seasons, cape-of-justice, fortune-cord, ancestral-embrace). The lane is live and populated; nothing needs inventing.

(3) OVERLAY COLLISION + NO DURABILITY. The fix writes only public/core.json, but `items` is NOT in import-core-v2's CARRY_WHOLESALE (scripts/import-core-v2.mjs:62-65 — only modes/stances/runes/services/followers/pets/animalCompanions/familiarAbilities/companionSpecializations/vehicles), so `npm run data` drops it. Worse, scripts/data/effect-backfill.json ALREADY holds a row for the exact same category/id/FIELD: {category:"items", id:"borts-blessing", field:"passiveEffects", value:{skills:{diplomacy:1}}}. A backfill row is "an ABSOLUTE assignment" (scripts/apply-backfill-now.mjs header), so replaying the overlay — even the fast `node scripts/apply-backfill-now.mjs` — overwrites passiveEffects wholesale and silently deletes the added languageChoices. The finding chose the one field that collides.

The corrected fix uses the daily-choice lane on the item, writes core.json AND a non-colliding overlay row (field `choice`), and touches no types and no passiveEffects.
---- CORRECTED FIX:
Deliver clause 1 through the DAILY-CHOICE lane on the item, not through passiveEffects. Leave passiveEffects ({skills:{diplomacy:1}}) and the FEAT_SITUATIONAL star untouched, and make NO change to src/rules/types.ts (ItemBase already declares `choice?: FeatChoiceDef` at types.ts:3479).

1) public/core.json — set items['borts-blessing'].choice (so the app sees it now; file is stored MINIFIED, write it back minified):
{
  "flag": "bortsLanguage",
  "prompt": "Language granted by Bort's Blessing",
  "kind": "open",
  "daily": true,
  "from": { "type": "language", "grantLanguage": true },
  "note": "Chosen each morning as part of your daily preparations and known until you prepare again, so it can't be used as a prerequisite for a permanent character option. The ring grants a COMMON language; rarer ones are listed with their rarity. Languages you already speak are not listed."
}

2) scripts/data/effect-backfill.json — DURABILITY. `items` is not in import-core-v2's CARRY_WHOLESALE, so append a row (via scripts/lib/write-backfill.mjs's writeBackfill, which preserves the stored 1-space-indent + CRLF format; do NOT hand-write JSON.stringify(…, null, 2)):
{ "category": "items", "id": "borts-blessing", "field": "choice", "value": { …the object above… } }
Field `choice` does NOT collide with the existing {items, borts-blessing, passiveEffects} row at ~line 3350; do not touch that row, and do not fold languageChoices into it (a backfill row is an absolute assignment and would be overwritten/overwrite).

3) Nothing else is required — the readers are already live for items: ownedDailyChoiceRecords (derive.ts:1209-1212, pushes equipped||worn||invested items) → dailyChoicesFor (dailyChoices.ts) renders the Rest-sheet picker via openChoiceOptions (openChoice.ts:134-137) → dailyChoiceGrants (derive.ts:1636-1638) emits {grantsLanguages:[answer]} → applyPlayState (play.ts:552-563) surfaces it as dailyLanguages. Bort's Blessing is usage "worn" with the invested trait, so it qualifies on both gates.

KNOWN, ACCEPTED CAVEAT (state it in the note, do not code around it): openChoiceOptions for type 'language' lists ALL languages and shows rarity as a right-hand note rather than filtering to common (openChoice.ts:134-137) — the same behaviour Ancestral Linguistics ships, consistent with the repo's shown-not-filtered rarity rule. The "common" restriction therefore rides in `note`, which is why the note text above names it.

OPTIONAL, NOT PART OF THE FIX: scripts/scan-daily-prep.mjs will not pin this record — its DAILY regex requires "as part of YOUR daily preparations" (print says "their") and its TEMP_GRANT regex expects "become fluent in one … language" (print says "understand, read, and speak one common language"). Widening those two patterns is a separate, deliberate change; if it is made, this record must land as OK, not TODO.

1. src/rules/types.ts InnateSpellGrant: do NOT add `frozenRank`. Instead amend the `rank` doc (line 2131-2133) — it no longer says "Cantrip/at-will grants ignore this" — and add ONE field:
   /** The at-will cantrip auto-heighten applies only when the character is a spellcaster of THIS
    *  tradition; otherwise `rank` is the frozen cast rank. Ring of Minor Arcana: "Each is cast as a
    *  1st-rank spell. If you are an arcane spellcaster, these can instead be heightened to the rank
    *  of your cantrips." Player Core p.298 allows it: innate cantrips auto-heighten "unless
    *  otherwise specified". */

2. src/rules/build.ts:6583 — replace
     if (base === 0) return g.atWill || g.rank == null ? 0 : g.rank;
   with
     if (base === 0) {
       if (g.rank == null) return 0;
       // "If you are an arcane spellcaster" — innate spells never make you one (Player Core p.298),
       // so this asks the SLOT casters only. `spellcasting` already holds the class entries here
       // (`caster` is derived from it at 6367); `spellTraditions` is declared 60 lines below and
       // carries the grant's own tradition, so it is both a TDZ error and the wrong question.
       const casts = g.heightenIfTradition
         && spellcasting.some((e) => (e.type === 'prepared' || e.type === 'spontaneous') && e.tradition === g.heightenIfTradition);
       return casts ? 0 : g.rank;
     }
   Nothing else moves: no other at-will rank-0 grant we ship sets `rank`.
3. Overlay — EDIT the EXISTING scripts/data/effect-backfill.json row {category:'items', id:'ring-of-minor-arcana', field:'innateSpells'} (do not append a second row for that category/id/field), adding to each of the three grants: "rank": 1, "heightenIfTradition": "arcane".     for (const r of propertyRuneDefs(inv, db)) read(r.passiveEffects /*, r.name for the Detail variant */);

    const br = bodyRuneDef(c, db); if (br) read(br.passiveEffects /*, br.name */);
⚠ Use the loop's existing `itemInUse(inv)` gate, NOT the `inv.worn && inv.invested` gate at derive.ts:4907 — that pattern is armour-shaped and a held clan dagger is `equipped`, never `worn`, so copying it verbatim would keep the bonus at zero. 3. SCOPE. Ruling B is directly on point: "a flat bonus the rules restrict belongs in `situational` with the restriction in its `when`, not in passiveEffects." A star is the lane, and the project already ruled that way on this exact sentence at :3223. "A prose description is delivery" covers play-time PROCEDURE text; a restricted numeric bonus is the one shape the star lane exists for, and accepting prose here would retroactively invalidate the sibling entry.

4. 1. This is NOT unmodellable: the lane exists and this feat's own sibling uses it. `spellNotes` (declared src/rules/types.ts:324/381, owner principle N2 documented at types.ts:369-380) is read at src/rules/build.ts:5442/5459 → Character.spellNotes → src/rules/explain.ts:688 spellNotesFor → rendered under the granting feat's name inside the spell's own description. core.json feats/confounding-image — same Spell Trickster archetype, same Grand Bazaar page range, same "you can cast this spell with its duration changed to sustained up to 1 minute" shape — carries exactly such a note on spellId 'mirror-image'.

If you do, it gains a Heightened (3rd) entry — the disk holds up to 10 Bulk; the spell no longer ends if YOU ride atop it (it still ends if any other creature does, including one riding on you); while you ride it its capacity becomes you plus Bulk equal to the maximum you could carry unencumbered; each time you Sustain you can direct it along the ground up to 30 feet or your Speed, whichever is slower; it can move over liquids as long as it ends its movement on solid ground, and the spell ends if it ends on a surface that can\'t support it.'}] on feats/disk-rider. If you do, add the following heightened entry to its effects." followed by the full **Heightened (3rd)** block (10 Bulk; no longer ends if YOU ride atop it, still ends if any other creature does incl. one riding on you; capacity becomes you plus Bulk equal to your unencumbered max; direct it 30 ft or your Speed, whichever is slower, each Sustain; over liquids if it ends on solid ground; ends if it ends on a surface that can't support it). Quote and content check out.

CARRIER SWEEP (re-derived, not taken on trust): `grep -rn --include=*.ts --include=*.tsx --include=*.json "disk-rider" src/ scripts/data/` returns ZERO. public/core.json contains "disk-rider" exactly twice, both the record's own key/id — the record has only actionCost/archetype/prerequisites/aonId, no spellNotes, no situational, no recordMarks. scripts/data/effect-backfill.json has no row with id containing "disk"; consumable-modes.json and toggle-modes.json have no "disk". So "zero carriers" is TRUE.

ONE CORRECTION TO THE FINDING'S EVIDENCE: "Both printed clauses … reach nothing" is overstated. public/core-descriptions.json feats/disk-rider `d` carries BOTH clauses complete and verbatim, including the whole Heightened (3rd) block. What is actually missing is the N2 cross-surface: the clause never appears under the spell the player is looking at while casting. That is still a defect, because `spellNote` is a built lane whose stated purpose (types.ts:369-380, mechanic-lanes.md line 97) is exactly this — "leaving it on the feat alone puts it on a page nobody is looking at while casting" — and 92 shipped spellId notes use it. The same-archetype, same-book, same-shape sibling feats/confounding-image carries one on mirror-image without granting the spell either. Not covered by any DECIDED SCOPE ruling: no ally-only effect (F/N), no aura (M), no GM adjudication, no internal activity step, not a situational-star case.

NOT a WG-vs-print disagreement: WG's own feat row (wg-data.sql, feat 31003) encodes it as `injectText` type "spell" onto spell 6307, text identical to print. WG agrees with print AND with the lane. Not in work/owner-questions.json (only 3 open, none about disk/carryall). So neither ASK-OWNER trigger fires.

THE FIX AS WRITTEN IS WRONG, hence correctedFix. (a) spellId 'floating-disk' does not exist in public/core.json spells, so build.ts:5452 `if (!content.spells[spellId]) return;` drops the note silently and the authoring guard at scripts/apply-innate-spell-verification.mjs:270 throws "note on missing spell". All 92 current spellId notes resolve; this would be the only one that doesn't. (b) The finding's ⚠BLOCKER ("the spell is absent from our corpus… Import the spell, then author the note") is wrong on the remedy: Floating Disk was RENAMED, not dropped — AoN spell-124 "Floating Disk" carries remaster_id spell-1972 "Carryall" (Player Core 2 p.242), and spells/carryall IS in our corpus (edition remaster, rank 1, arcane/occult). The project's established practice for legacy-named spell references in legacy-era records is to key the note at the remaster record rather than import the retired name: azarketi-purification→cleanse-cuisine (print: purify food and drink), animal-magic→calm (print: calm emotions), nameless-anonymity→veil-of-privacy, cunning-tinker→mending. WG's 6307 is literally titled "Floating Disk (legacy)"; we do not carry that half. (c) The note text should say "this spell", not "Floating Disk's", because it renders inside Carryall's own description — matching the sibling's "you can cast this spell with its duration changed to sustained up to 1 minute". Vehicle is right: spellNotes is a live effect-backfill field (84 existing rows), no collision on feats/disk-rider, not a mode, not a description repair. Adjacent (separate claim, not fixed here): the record's descRef r:[{label:'Floating Disk', key:'spells'}] and its `d` text both still say "Floating Disk", which resolves to nothing in our spells bucket.
---- CORRECTED FIX:
Add ONE row to scripts/data/effect-backfill.json (no existing row for this category/id/field, so no collision):

{ "category": "feats", "id": "disk-rider", "field": "spellNotes", "value": [ { "spellId": "carryall", "note": "Disk Rider: when you cast this spell, you can modify its duration to be sustained, up to 10 minutes. If you do, add the following heightened entry to its effects. **Heightened (3rd)** The disk can hold up to 10 Bulk. The spell no longer ends if you ride atop the disk (though it does if any other creature rides atop it, including if the creature is riding on you). If you are riding the disk, it changes its capacity to hold you, plus additional Bulk equal to the maximum you could carry without being encumbered. If you ride atop the disk, you can direct it to move along the ground up to 30 feet or your Speed, whichever is slower, each time you Sustain the Spell. While you ride atop it, the disk can move over liquids as long as it ends its movement on solid ground. If it ends its movement on a surface that can't support it, the spell ends." } ] }

Changes from the finding's spec: spellId is 'carryall' (NOT 'floating-disk' — that id does not exist and build.ts:5452 would drop the note); the note opens "when you cast this spell" instead of naming Floating Disk, matching feats/confounding-image's shipped phrasing, because it renders inside the target spell's description; the "Disk Rider:" prefix stays (sibling house style, even though explain.ts also prints `from` = record name).

DROP the ⚠BLOCKER entirely — do NOT import a 'floating-disk' spell. The spell ships as its Remaster record spells/carryall; re-pointing a legacy-era record's clause at the remaster spell is the established pattern (azarketi-purification→cleanse-cuisine, animal-magic→calm, nameless-anonymity→veil-of-privacy).
